    def test_macos_preview_uses_modern_tk_without_changing_dry_run(tmp_path):
        import os
        import subprocess

        fake_bin = tmp_path / "bin"
        fake_bin.mkdir()
        uname = fake_bin / "uname"
        uname.write_text("#!/bin/sh\nprintf 'Darwin\\n'\n")
        uname.chmod(0o755)
        python = fake_bin / "python3.14"
        python.write_text(
            '#!/bin/sh\nif [ "$1" = -c ]; then exit 0; fi\n'
            'printf "dry=%s demo=%s\\n" "$BEAMO_WIPE_DRY_RUN" "$BEAMO_WIPE_DEMO"\n'
            'printf "%s\\n" "$@"\n'

        root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

                                text=True, capture_output=True, check=True)

usage: beamo-wipe [-h] [--demo] [--web] [--helper]
                  [--scenario {happy,empty,blocked,fail}] [--empty]
                  [--blocked] [--fail] [--plain-console] [--console]
                  [--accessible] [--fullscreen] [--dry-run]
                  [--lsblk-json LSBLK_JSON] [--boot-device BOOT_DEVICE]
                  [--version]

Guided front-end for nwipe. This is not a wipe engine. It will not erase disks
from inside Windows or macOS. On this computer, use --preview or ./preview
(fake disks).

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --demo, --preview     Tk window with fake disks. Nothing is erased.
  --web, --gallery      Open a browser click-through of the screens. Does not
                        wipe.
  --helper              Open the boot-menu helper page (does not wipe).
  --scenario {happy,empty,blocked,fail}
                        Preview disk list: happy, empty, blocked, or fail.
  --empty               Preview: only the Beamo USB.
  --blocked             Preview: cannot identify USB.
  --fail, --fail-demo   Preview a failed wipe.
  --plain-console       Use sequential text prompts without curses screen
                        redraws.
  --console             Use the keyboard console UI.
  --accessible          Use the Linux GTK screen-reader view.
  --fullscreen          Fill the screen (live USB).
  --dry-run             Do not invoke nwipe.
  --lsblk-json LSBLK_JSON
                        Read disks from an lsblk JSON file.
  --boot-device BOOT_DEVICE
                        Override live-medium path (tests only).
  --version             show program's version number and exit
